================================================================
RENYXA COGNITIVE FOUNDATION, INC.
CLUE TRANSCRIPT v2.0 OFFICIAL HEADER
----------------------------------------------------------------
Header text is unalienated part of the CLUE Transcript.
Any upstream alteration invalidates downstream processing.

Each module MUST preserve its XREF identifier
in format [DOCID|NNNNN].
================================================================
Syntax:
d:x# <SEGMENTS>

x ∈ {t, s}
t = transition present
s = statica only

d = diegetic line
n = non-diegetic line

Valid line prefixes:
d:t#
d:s#
n:t#
n:s#

Atomic predication segments:

- Agents / bearers
+A  = active initiator / emitter of transition
-A  = affected bearer / patient / entity with which the transition happens
O   = object / theme / recipient / bearer of stative predication

- Quantified agents / bearers
+AQ = quantified active initiator
-AQ = quantified affected bearer
OQ  = quantified object / theme / quantified bearer of stative predication

- Predicate segments
T   = transition / action / event / process
D   = stative predicate / declaration / fixed relation

Circumstantial layer uses script-style envelopes:

tm| ... |mt = time
lc| ... |cl = location
mn| ... |nm = manner
pr| ... |rp = purpose
cu| ... |uc = cause
ac| ... |ca = accompany
sp| ... |ps = scope
dg| ... |gd = degree
cn| ... |nc = concession
cm| ... |mc = comparison
cd| ... |dc = condition
pq| ... |qp = prerequisite
rs| ... |sr = consequence

Operational rules:

1. L1 predication uses only:
   +A, -A, O, +AQ, -AQ, OQ, T, D

2. Quantifier never lives as a standalone segment.
   Quantifier is encoded only inside a quantified participant:
   +AQ / -AQ / OQ

3. L2 circumstantial envelopes may contain L1 segments.
   L1 segments do not contain L2 envelopes.

4. L3 is a line prefix only.
   It does not segment the sentence internally.

5. If all markup is removed, the underlying sentence surface
   must remain byte-identical to the source line.

================================================================
END OF THE OFFICIAL HEADER
